# Infinite Kitchen - the hand-written core (restart, 2026-10-04).
#
# One combo per line:   A + B = Result [kind]
# Pairs are unordered. [kind] goes on any one of a result's lines; it
# defaults to ingredient. Tools are the ones in the room: Stove, Oven, Pot,
# Knife, Blender, Clock, Fridge - seven, all usable from the start. Players
# discover food, never tools. The Stove does all pan cooking (fry, grill,
# sear); the Clock is time in every form (age, ferment, grow, spoil).
#
# Elements: Water, Grain, Plant, Animal, Salt, Sugar.
#
# When it isn't obvious what a pair makes, think of everything food can be,
# not just cooking: groceries, snacks, candy, brands, restaurants and their
# iconic menu items, baking, drinks. Animal + Candy is nothing in a recipe,
# but it's Gummy Bears in the candy aisle. Use that range to find the answer
# that clicks; don't go looking for ways to squeeze brands in.

## The elements with each other
Water + Water = Ocean
Grain + Grain = Wheat
Plant + Plant = Tree
Animal + Animal = Farm
Sugar + Sugar = Candy [dish]
Salt + Salt = Rock Salt
Water + Grain = Rice
Water + Plant = Vegetables
Water + Animal = Fish
Water + Sugar = Syrup
Grain + Animal = Hen
Grain + Sugar = Cereal
Plant + Animal = Cow
Plant + Sugar = Fruit
Plant + Salt = Seaweed
Animal + Sugar = Bee

## Second step: where the real food comes from
Farm + Plant = Vegetables
Plant + Boiling Water = Tea [drink]
Hen + Clock = Egg
Hen + Knife = Chicken
Cow + Water = Milk
Cow + Knife = Beef
Bee + Tree = Honey
Tree + Sugar = Maple Syrup
Tree + Fruit = Apple
Vegetables + Fruit = Tomato
Grain + Blender = Flour

## Cooking
Flour + Water = Dough
Dough + Oven = Bread [dish]
Bread + Stove = Toast [dish]
Toast + Stove = Burnt [trash]
Milk + Clock = Cheese
Egg + Stove = Fried Egg [dish]
Egg + Flour = Batter
Batter + Stove = Pancakes [dish]
Tomato + Pot = Tomato Sauce
Dough + Tomato Sauce = Pizza [dish]
Bread + Cheese = Grilled Cheese [dish]
Beef + Stove = Steak [dish]
Fish + Rice = Sushi [dish]

## Surprises: only where nothing real fits
Fish + Candy = Swedish Fish [dish]

# Cuisines (2026-10-08): seven cookbooks on the shelf. Each unlocks the first
# time you make any dish or drink from that cuisine (the lists below), and
# those get a card striped in the cuisine's colors. Then drag the cookbook
# onto food for that cuisine's real version. Real dishes only - when a
# cuisine has no version, leave the pair open.
#
# Only clear regional dishes are listed. Generic food and grocery brands stay
# white, and very common ones (French Fries, Chicken Nuggets) are left off so
# a cookbook isn't too easy to unlock.
#############################################################################

## Feast (2026-10-13): dishes that go together
# The opposite of Slop: two dishes that belong on the same table make
# something bigger. Finger foods make a Platter, two Platters (or a holiday
# table) make a Feast, and Feasts make a Buffet. Only pairs that really get
# served together - this is never a default for any two dishes.

# Finger foods: a Platter
Buffalo Wings + Mozzarella Sticks = Platter [dish]
Mozzarella Sticks + Onion Rings = Platter
Deviled Eggs + Pigs in a Blanket = Platter
Charcuterie Board + Shrimp Cocktail = Platter
Charcuterie Board + Cheese and Crackers = Platter
Chips and Guac + Chips and Salsa = Platter
Pakora + Samosa = Platter
Egg Rolls + Spring Rolls = Platter

# Small plates have their own names where they come from
Falafel + Hummus and Pita = Mezze [dish]
Baba Ganoush + Tabbouleh = Mezze
Platter + Middle Eastern = Mezze
Bao + Potstickers = Dim Sum [dish]
Platter + East Asian = Dim Sum
Platter + Indian = Thali [dish]

# Two platters, or a holiday table: a Feast
Platter + Platter = Feast [dish]
Ham + Roast Turkey = Feast
Pumpkin Pie + Thanksgiving Dinner = Feast
Brisket + Ribs = Feast
Platter + Barbecue = Feast

# A cookbook sets the table its own way
Feast + American = Thanksgiving Dinner
Feast + Middle Eastern = Mansaf
Feast + East Asian = Hot Pot [dish]

# The biggest table there is
Feast + Feast = Buffet [dish]
Feast + Platter = Buffet
